tool_result: STT_FUT_SELL = 0.0002            # 0.02% on the SELL side notional (futures)
STT_OPT_SELL = 0.0010            # 0.10% on the SELL side premium (options)
TXN_FUT = 0.0000173              # NSE 0.00173% of futures turnover
TXN_OPT = 0.0003503              # NSE 0.03503% of option premium turnover
SEBI_RATE = 0.000001             # Rs 10 per crore
GST_RATE = 0.18                  # 18% on (brokerage + transaction + SEBI)
STAMP_FUT_BUY = 0.00002          # 0.002% on the BUY side notional (futures)
STAMP_OPT_BUY = 0.00003          # 0.003% on the BUY side premium (options)


def _leg_cost(turnover, kind, side):
    """Zerodha statutory+brokerage for ONE leg. kind in {'fut','opt'}, side in {'buy','sell'}."""
    brokerage = min(FO_BROKERAGE_PER_ORDER, FO_BROKERAGE_PCT * turnover)
    stt = ((STT_FUT_SELL if kind == "fut" else STT_OPT_SELL) * turnover) if side == "sell" else 0.0
    txn = (TXN_FUT if kind == "fut" else TXN_OPT) * turnover
    sebi = SEBI_RATE * turnover
    gst = GST_RATE * (brokerage + txn + sebi)
    stamp = ((STAMP_FUT_BUY if kind == "fut" else STAMP_OPT_BUY) * turnover) if side == "buy" else 0.0
    return {"turnover": round(turnover, 2), "brokerage": round(brokerage, 2), "stt": round(stt, 2),
            "txn": round(txn, 2), "sebi": round(sebi, 4), "gst": round(gst, 2), "stamp": round(stamp, 2),
            "total": round(brokerage + stt + txn + sebi + gst + stamp, 2)}


def fo_parity_roundtrip_cost(ce_prem, pe_prem, fut_price, lot_size, lots=1, call_rich=True):
    """Full statutory+brokerage cost of a put-call-parity arb ROUND TRIP: three legs (CE, PE, future),
    each traded at entry and exit (6 orders). call_rich (C-P too high) -> entry: sell CE, buy PE, buy FUT;
    exit reverses. Returns (total_rupees, per_leg_breakdown). This does NOT include the bid-ask spread —
    that is a market cost, not a broker charge, and is accounted separately."""
    ce_to = ce_prem * lot_size * lots
    pe_to = pe_prem * lot_size * lots
    fut_to = fut_price * lot_size * lots
=== cost_readout ===
def cost_readout():
    """One place that prints WHERE every governing money number comes from (R-HOLD-AND-FIX 4)."""
    mhs = measured_half_spread(); mms = measured_max_spread_frac(); mc = measured_cushion()
    return {
        "statutory_round_trip": {"value": COST_STATUTORY_RT, "source": "typed+cited (Zerodha+NSE published rates)"},
        "half_spread": {"value": half_spread(), "formula": "median(observed spread frac)/2",
                        "source": "MEASURED" if mhs is not None else "cited fallback %.4f (COLD-START; no depth yet)" % HALF_SPREAD},
        "cost_round_trip": {"value": COST_STATUTORY_RT + 2 * half_spread(),
                            "formula": "statutory + 2*half_spread"},
        "max_spread_frac": {"value": mms, "formula": "0.95-quantile(observed spread frac)",
                            "source": "MEASURED" if mms is not None else "none yet -> moment light fail-closed"},
        "cushion_M": {"value": cushion(), "formula": "median Wilson half-width over blotter fire counts",
                      "source": "MEASURED" if mc is not None else "cited fallback %.3f (COLD-START)" % CUSHION_M},
        "day_halt": {"source": "circuit_breaker.worst_backtest_streak (measured) + book_guard measured worst DD; "
                     "the typed DAY_HALT_R in the retired intraday loop no longer governs any order"},
    }

A design note only: it records how the lead (the\nautonomous builder session) would run directly on this laptop, and the constraints that already bind it. Goal: Codex CLI signed in, proven by a non-interactive run; owner taps at most one link.

If the network blocks OpenAI, post ONE Telegram line: "Codex: laptop network blocks OpenAI — switch to phone hotspot and reply 'hotspot on'"; poll 15 minutes; no reply → mark Codex UNAVAILABLE and continue to Part 2. ===== PART 3 · R-WATCHERS =====
Goal: failures that happen while nobody is committing are caught by the machine; new code never runs first at 06:00.
1. Freshness watch inside the existing watchdog process (no new process): every cycle fetch the public status.html; written time older than a few status cycles (threshold measured from the writer's cadence, recorded in docs/EYES.md) → ONE line "SITE FROZEN since <written time>", then hourly until fresh. In market hours, any recorder stream whose last row is older than its measured cadence → ONE line "STREAM SILENT <name> since <time>".
2. Message-rate guard, desk-wide: identical Telegram text posts once, then at most hourly; more than a measured normal count of lines per minute → ONE line "MESSAGE STORM <sender>" and that sender is muted until cleared.
3. Nightly dry run as a scheduled job in START_E1_STACK's schedule (no new process): every night after build hours — tag the last verified commit; restart through restart_stack.py; wait one status cycle; check expected processes, public page freshness, NOW line; PASS → ONE line "NIGHT DRY RUN PASS <commit>"; FAIL → revert to the tag, restart with it, ONE line "NIGHT DRY RUN FAILED — reverted to <tag>: <reason>" plus LEAD RULING NEEDED. The 08:15 gate stays as the second check.
4. Tests for each; commit per step; all existing tests green. Record every watcher in docs/EYES.md (sees what · max age · what freezes it · what still needs the owner).
5. Report to docs/reports first, one Telegram line, self-flag line.

===== PART 4 · R-VERIFIER v2 =====
Goal: no build step counts as done until an independent verifier has RUN it and written PASS with observed evidence to the mirror; every lead ruling is scored against the lead checklist in public.
1. Brain ladder, read from docs/VERIFIER-BRAIN.md: (a) Codex CLI non-interactive with ChatGPT sign-in; (b) Codex with an OpenAI API key if one is ever placed in E1-DESK (never in the repo); (c) a different vendor's model by API if a key is placed the same way; (d) the existing supervisor mechanism — a fresh independent Claude run — with this ruling's template. Use the highest rung available tonight; the launcher re-checks the ladder each run so signing into Codex later upgrades it with no other change. The verifier goes live tonight on whichever rung is available.
2. Template docs/VERIFIER-TEMPLATE.md: receives the ruling text, the commit diff, the builder's claimed step; reads only repo + mirror; never edits code; a claim without evidence is FAIL.
3. It runs, never just reads, per docs/VERIFY-MAP.md: full test suite always; scripts → dry mode; hooks → simulated tool call plus simulated halt-and-clear; site → fetch the public URL and read its written time; desk processes → list them; notifiers → watch msg_log a few minutes after the change, no repeated line; trading path (signal, order, ledger, exit, runner) → end-to-end rehearsal: hash the live ledgers, run the real runner in rehearsal context with one synthetic signal for a ready stock, assert a paper order row in the rehearsal ledger, the exit logic closes it, the would-be Telegram text is in the capture file and nothing was sent, live ledger hashes unchanged.
4. Output docs/reports/verify-<commit>.md — PASS or FAIL, each claim with the command run and the output line; rendered on Reports; ONE line "VERIFY PASS|FAIL <ruling step> <commit>".
5. Gate: the auto-continue hook refuses the next queue item until verify-PASS exists for the last commit. FAIL → its text is the builder's next instruction. Three FAILs on one item → parked, LEAD RULING NEEDED. Verifier unavailable on every rung → "VERIFY UNAVAILABLE", gate closed. The gate always allows reads, git commit, docs/dispatches, journal, clear.
6. Independence: verifier config and launch live in E1-DESK outside the repo, so the build session cannot switch it off. Supervisor keeps scope and provenance only.
7. Lead checklist: docs/LEAD-CHECKLIST.md (Part 5 writes it). The supervisor scores every archived ruling against it (self-flag line, no typed ETA, "live" only with a cited proof, "where this can fail" line, one path, engines by name) and writes pass/fail per rule to the Queue page beside the ruling, plus the lead's hit rate: rulings handed vs proven live, from docs/dispatches and the verifier reports.
8. Proof of the verifier: a throwaway branch commit that breaks one test → FAIL naming that test; a real commit → PASS; a throwaway change on the trading path → the rehearsal runs and live ledger hashes are shown unchanged; one archived ruling shows its checklist score on Queue. Delete the branch after. No test touches a ledger or sends any Telegram line except the verifier's own.
9. Report to docs/reports first, one Telegram line, self-flag line.

===== PART 5 · R-LEAD-FILES =====
1. docs/LEAD-CHECKLIST.md, verbatim:
 1. Eyes first: open the mirror, state its written time against the owner's phone time. Can't open it → say EYES GAP, never rule blind.
 2. One ruling in flight. Nothing new until the verifier's PASS for the last one is on the mirror.
 3. Report = claim. Verifier's run = exam. Never accept "done" from a report.
 4. "Live" only when observed in the running loop by a session that didn't build it — line-cited in the loop the login launches, plus a dry run through it. Otherwise "built".
 5. Builder ≠ verifier, different model. No session audits its own work.
 6. Money math at design time: fees, fills, spread, exits, caps. Costs inside every exam.
 7. Foresee where it breaks: every ruling carries a "where this can fail" line, written before the build.
 8. No typed numbers. Measured only. ETAs machine-measured or "unmeasured".
 9. Demos and tests never reach Telegram or a live ledger.
 10. Core untouchable: login, launch, loop, orders change only through the nightly dry run with the 08:15 auto-revert. Plug-ins unlimited, each proven before the next.
 11. Every dispatch: one path; no new process or hook unless it replaces one; archived to docs/dispatches before work; ends with the self-flag line.
 12. Every observability ruling states per watcher: sees what · how old · what freezes it · what still needs the owner.
 13. Engines by the owner's names. Blocks labelled → BUILD or → AUDIT. One paste a day outside emergencies.
 14. Failed strategy → probation. Luck correction counts every trial, not the winners.
2. docs/ACCEPTANCE.md — five checks, each marked PASS only by the verifier or an audit report on the mirror, never by the builder: (1) mirror written time under two minutes old when fetched; (2) an owner → AUDIT from Telegram lands as a report with no session opened; (3) a broken commit returns FAIL naming the test; (4) nightly dry run passes on two consecutive nights; (5) five clean live-paper sessions observed through the mirror. The BUILD beat shows "ACCEPTED n/5". No parked item starts while ACCEPTED is below 5/5.
3. docs/LEARNING-TRACK.md — parked until ACCEPTED 5/5, in this order, each step through the exam with costs, luck correction counting every trial, and a minimum-cases floor: live paper proven → point-in-time universe (O1, lifts the survivorship haircut) → one feature table (a row per stock per minute, features only from what was known at that minute, append-only) → audit of the exam against de Prado (luck correction counts all trials; walk-forward with an embargo gap equal to the label horizon) → meta-labeling on the existing signals → cross-sectional model. Standing habit: the owner pastes a title or link; the lead turns it into one exam check; an audit says whether the exam already does it; if not it joins this file.
4. Commit; one Telegram line; self-flag line.

def run(notifier=None, now=None):
    """Steps 1–5 with proofs. Returns the proof dict. Refuses unless guard() passes."""
    now = now or _now()
    if not guard(now):
        return {"refused": "guard failed (needs after 15:30 IST AND a HEAD commit touching the launch path)",
                "after_1530": after_1530(now), "launch_path_touched": launch_path_touched()}
    self_pid = os.getpid()
    killed = stop(exclude_pid=self_pid)                       # (1)
    time.sleep(2)
    remaining = [p for p in _ps_list() if p["pid"] != self_pid]  # (1) proof
    start()                                                   # (2)
    time.sleep(6)
    up = verify_up(exclude_pid=self_pid)                      # (3)
    armed = armed_checks()                                    # (3)
    au = audit()                                              # (4)
    hhmm = _now().strftime("%H:%M")
    all_up = all(up[n]["up"] and up[n]["py310"] for n in UP_NAMES)
    line = ("STACK: restarted %s — token_ping/watchdog/site_loop/supervisor up, 06:00 link armed, "
            "08:15 gate armed, audit %s" % (hhmm, "PASS" if au["pass"] else "FAIL")) if all_up else \
           ("STACK: restart %s — NOT all up: %s" % (hhmm, {n: up[n]["up"] for n in UP_NAMES}))
    try:
        (notifier or _notify)(line)                          # (5)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {"killed": killed, "remaining_after_stop": remaining, "up": up, "armed": armed,
            "audit": au, "line": line}


def _notify(msg):
    from scripts import notify
    notify.notify(msg)


def _demo():
    # guard refuses before 15:30 regardless of the commit
    morning = _now().replace(hour=9, minute=0)
    assert guard(morning) is False
    r = run(notifier=lambda m: None, now=morning)
    assert r.get("refused"), r
    # after 15:30 the time half passes (launch-path half depends on the real HEAD commit)
    assert after_1530(_now().replace(hour=16, minute=0)) is True
    print("restart_stack demo OK: guard refuses before 15:30; proofs shape intact.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
        _demo()
    elif "--run" in sys.argv:
        print(json.dumps(run(), indent=1, default=str))
    else:
        print("use --run (after 15:30, launch-path commit) or --demo")

        try:

        except Exception:
            pass  # a watchdog must never crash
        try:

        except Exception:
            pass

            pass

tool_result: def notify(message: str, timeout: float = 10.0) -> bool:
    """Send one plain-language Telegram ping, prefixed [E1]. SEND ONLY.

    Returns True on a 200 from Telegram, False on any absence/error (never raises).
    The token and chat id are never printed or logged, on success or failure.
    """
    # R-NO-DEMO-TELEGRAM: a demo/test/dry-run message is DROPPED at the notifier and logged — never sent.
    _why = _demo_context(message)
    if _why:
        _drop_demo(message, _why)
        return False
    if _rate_limited(message):                # R-STOP-SPAM: identical text posts once, then at most hourly
        _log_send(message, "RATE-LIMITED")
        return False
    _log_message(message)                     # amendment 3: every real outbound message -> the Messages log
    try:
        from scripts import msg_log            # R-EYES-COMPLETE §2: the mirror's incoming/outgoing log
        msg_log.outgoing(message)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        from scripts import heartbeat            # R-E1-TIMER+CLOCK: update the public heartbeat the
        heartbeat.touch(message)                 # alarm page reads (⏱ / 🙋 / session-finished), IST.
    except Exception:
        pass
    # R-E1-SIREN-WINDOW: on a 🙋 (needs-you / stall summon), OPEN the visible always-on-top alarm window
    # on the laptop and ring it (never headless). Dedupes; best-effort; never fatal to the send.
    try:
        if "\U0001f64b" in str(message):
            from scripts import ring as ring_window
            ring_window.ring(str(message)[:160])
    except Exception:
        pass
    # R-E1-BOOK: refresh the Desktop project book on every ✅, detached + best-effort (never blocks the send)
    try:
        if "✅" in str(message):
            import subprocess
            repo = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
            flags = 0x08000000 if os.name == "nt" else 0     # CREATE_NO_WINDOW

149:def _rate_limited(message, window_s=3600):
def _rate_limited(message, window_s=3600):
    """R-STOP-SPAM: desk-wide — identical text sends once, then at most once per hour. Different text (the beat,
    the status line, etc.) is never affected. Best-effort; a store error never blocks a send."""
    import hashlib
    import time
    key = hashlib.md5(str(message).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    now = time.time()
    try:
        seen = json.load(open(_RATE_STORE, encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        seen = {}
    seen = {k: v for k, v in seen.items() if now - v < window_s}     # prune
    if seen.get(key) is not None and now - seen[key] < window_s:
        return True                                                 # identical text within the hour -> drop
    seen[key] = now
    try:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(_RATE_STORE), exist_ok=True)
        json.dump(seen, open(_RATE_STORE, "w", encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return False

# Audit: do tomorrow's LIVE paper trades match the exam that produced `LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json`?

The single live order path is `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py` (its own docstring: *"THE SINGLE ORDER PATH — the 20-day book runner. This is the ONLY loop that may write a paper order."* — book20_runner.py:1). The exam that produced the pattern set is `scripts/exams_real.py` ("R-EXAMS-REAL"), promoted into the JSON by `scripts/live_pattern_set.py`.

Bottom line up front: **the live loop does NOT reproduce the certified management.** It hard-codes a 20-day hold, a 1×ATR stop, and always-on trailing/break-even/partial — while every pattern in `LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json` was certified with a 40- or 60-day hold, a **2×ATR** stop, and **trail=false** ("fixed stop and target"). Details per your five points below.

---

## 1. SIZING

**Live size = flat 1R = ₹1,000, sized so 1R ≈ one ATR of the stock.**

- `engine1_rebuild/money.py:12` — `PAPER_R_RUPEES = 1000.0  # 1R = the rupee risk per call`
- The order-writing function `fill_at_touch` does **not** compute a quantity or rupee size at all — the ledger row carries only `entry/stop/atr`, no size field (book20_runner.py:170-173). Size is materialised only in the card:
  - `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py:570` — `qty = max(1, round(money.PAPER_R_RUPEES / atr)) if atr else 1  # size so ~1R = the stock's own wiggle`
- The design explicitly keeps the order path flat and does **not** use the Kelly/adaptive sizer:
  - `engine1_rebuild/desk.py:20-23` — *"The 20-day book sizes every fire at a flat, shrunk 1R (standing architecture), so book20_runner never calls position_risk_rupees."*
- `adaptive_sizing.py` is **record-only** — printed on the card but never sizes the order:
  - `engine1_rebuild/adaptive_sizing.py:14` — *"adaptive never sizes on a number it does not have. RECORD-ONLY."*
  - It is only appended to the card text: book20_runner.py:583 — `card += "\nsizing · FLAT ₹%.0f · %s" % (money.PAPER_R_RUPEES, AS.explain(adaptive))`
- `flat_floor` scales with equity but for a fresh book equals ₹1,000: `adaptive_sizing.py:29-35` — `R_FRAC = PAPER_R_RUPEES / NOTIONAL_CAPITAL` (i.e. 1000/200000 = 0.5%).
- E10/E11 differ only by a sizing weight on the **same** fill (see #3), never a different price: `engine_book.py:87` — `risk = round(w * ACC.R_RUPEES, 2)`.

**The exam is pure-R, no rupee size.** `net_per_trade_R` is the mean of net-R across walk-forward trades, with no position size assumed:
- `scripts/exams_real.py:131` — `mean_net = statistics.mean(nets) if nets else 0.0`
- `scripts/exams_real.py:134` — `"mean_net_R": round(mean_net, 4)` (this is what becomes `net_per_trade_R`)
- Promotion mapping: `scripts/live_pattern_set.py:41` — `"net_per_trade_R": s.get("mean_net_R")`
- Each trade's R comes from `paper.simulate_trade(...)` and `net_R = gross_R - _cost_R(chk)` (exams_real.py:120-126) — R units only, no rupees.

**Caveat — the definition of "1R" differs between exam and live.** In the exam, 1R = `stop_atr` ATR (the certified stop, 2.0): `engine1_rebuild/paper.py:187` — `partial_R = 1.5 / sa` and paper.py:169-170 *"1R = stop_atr ATR"*. In the live loop, 1R = 1 ATR (stop is 1×ATR, see #2). So a given `net_per_trade_R` from the exam is measured against a 2-ATR risk unit, but the live book's ₹1,000 is risked over 1 ATR — the rupee meaning of the certified R is not preserved.

---

## 2. EXIT logic — three mismatches vs the certified patterns

Live exits are applied by `manage_position` and the initial stop by `fill_at_touch`. The runner passes the **module constant `H = 20`** as the hold, not the pattern's horizon.

- `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py:39` — `H = 20   # the book's horizon in trading days`
- `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py:401-402` — the fill call passes `H` (not `c["horizon_days"]`) as `hold_ticks`:
  `fill_at_touch(tid, c["stock"], side, (quotes or {}).get(c["stock"]), _atr_of(c), H, ...)`
  — even though the candidate carries the certified horizon at book20_runner.py:339 (`"horizon_days": mgmt.get("horizon", H)`), that value is never used for the fill.
- Initial stop = **1×ATR**: `book20_runner.py:163` — `stop_px = round(touch - float(atr), 2) if long else round(touch + float(atr), 2)   # 1×ATR = 1R`
- Management = **1×ATR stop + break-even + trail + half-partial + time exit**, always on:
  - `book20_runner.py:185` — `stop = entry - atr if long else entry + atr           # 1×ATR = 1R (NOT the measured MAE stop)`
  - `book20_runner.py:197` — trail: `new_stop = max(stop, entry, trail) if long else min(stop, entry, trail)`
  - `book20_runner.py:202-205` — half partial at +1.5 ATR
  - `book20_runner.py:227` — time exit: `if i + 1 &gt;= hold_ticks:`
- The docstring itself flags the divergence: `book20_runner.py:12-14` — *"a 1xATR stop (NOT the measured MAE-percentile stop), break-even, trail, half-partial, time exit."*

**Compare to `LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json`:** every one of the 5 patterns has `stop_atr: 2.0`, `trail: false`, and horizon 40 or 60 (LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json:9-49), with management text *"hold up to 60 days, stop 2 times the stock's own daily swing, fixed stop and target"* (e.g. line 12). So the live loop diverges on **all three** management dimensions:
  - **Horizon:** live holds 20 days; patterns are 40/60.
  - **Stop:** live 1×ATR; certified 2×ATR.
  - **Trail:** live always trails + takes a partial + moves to break-even; certified `trail:false` (fixed stop and target).

The exam actually simulated the certified variant (2×ATR, trail flag) via `paper.simulate_trade(..., manage=trail, stop_atr=stop_ps)` (exams_real.py:120-121, with `stop_ps` widened by `stop/2.0` at exams_real.py:94). The live loop does not read any of those parameters.

---

## 3. ORDER + FILL functions — one shared path; E10/E11 do NOT fill independently

- **One order-row writer:** `paper.append_rows` — book20_runner.py:431-433 (`_append_orders` → `paper.append_rows(rows)`), defined at `engine1_rebuild/paper.py:278`.
- **One live fill-pricer:** `fill_at_touch` — `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py:144`. (A second function, `live_20d.limit_at_mid_fill` at live_20d.py:32, exists but is only the "would-be" mid experiment — book20_runner.py:8-9 *"the mid fill is only ledgered as a would-be column"*.)
- **Exit pricer:** `manage_position` — book20_runner.py:176, exits written back via `paper.append_rows` (book20_runner.py:535-538).
- **E10 and E11 share E1's fill — they do not fork it.** `engine_book.py:9-11` — *"The order path stays the flat book's (same locks, fills, ~20 bps costs, exits, 20-position cap — nothing here forks the fill code); this module only RECORDS what each engine's sizing would have made."* They mirror the same fire via `engine_book.record_entry`/`record_exit` (called from book20_runner.py:728-733 and :758-762) using the entry/stop/`result_R` that E1's path produced; only the rupee multiplier (`weight × ₹1,000`) differs (engine_book.py:87, :107).

So: **one shared order function (`paper.append_rows`) and one shared live fill function (`fill_at_touch`), both owned by E1's book20_runner; E10/E11 record-only off the same fill.** No engine has its own order/fill path.

---

## 4. FILL PRICING — real top-of-book touch (ask/bid), not mid, not close

- `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py:151` — `touch = ask if long else bid`
- `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py:170` — the entry price written is the touch: `"entry": round(touch, 2)`
- The bid/ask come from the live top-of-book depth: book20_runner.py:135-137 (`_top` reads `depth.buy[0].price` / `depth.sell[0].price`).
- Mid is computed only as a "would-be" comparison, never the fill: book20_runner.py:157 (`mid = (bid + ask) / 2.0`) and :166 (`would_be_mid ... what resting at mid WOULD have saved`). Header confirms: book20_runner.py:8 *"FILL AT THE TOUCH (long@ask / short@bid) ... the mid fill is only ledgered as a would-be column."*
- **Skeptical note — the exam prices fills differently.** The certification enters at the **next daily open**, not the intraday touch: `engine1_rebuild/paper.py:98` — `e = signal_pos + 1  # next bar's open = the fill` and paper.py:101 `entry = o[e]`. Exits book at the daily bar's stop/target/close (paper.py:114-129, :190-213). So live = intraday ask/bid at signal time; exam = next-day open with daily-bar exits. The two fill models are not the same.

---

## 5. SPREAD CHECK WARM-UP

- **Warm-up threshold = 20 of the stock's OWN readings.** `engine1_rebuild/money.py:89` — `SPREAD_READINGS_MIN = 20`
- The measured ceiling returns `None` until a stock has ≥20 of its own readings, so its lock stays closed: `engine1_rebuild/money.py:139` — `if len(xs) &lt; SPREAD_READINGS_MIN:     # not enough of THIS stock's own readings -&gt; stock stays locked` → `return None`. Explicitly **no desk-wide fallback for the lock** (money.py:137).
- **The gate that decides HELD** is `desk.moment_light`. When the ceiling isn't measured yet it fails closed:
  - `engine1_rebuild/desk.py:49-52` — `ceiling = money.measured_max_spread_frac(key) ...` → `if ceiling is None: return False, (f"no MEASURED per-stock ceiling yet for {key or 'this name'} ({spread * 100:.2f}% spread unverifiable → locked, fail-closed)")`
  - and if measured but too wide: desk.py:53-54 — `"market too wide to fill ..."`.
- In the runner, a non-green moment → order not allowed → the fire is HELD and logged:
  - `book20_runner.py:390-391` (moment must be green for `gate.order_ok`), and the plain-language reason at `book20_runner.py:623` — `return "HELD — moment light not green (spread/depth)"`
  - HELD fires are logged to the site's held list, never Telegram: book20_runner.py:681 → `_log_held(...)`.

**Skeptical answer to your specific question:** yes, a signal **is** HELD when the spread data isn't ready (fewer than 20 readings → ceiling `None` → `moment_light` returns False → HELD). **But the HELD reason string does NOT name "warm-up" or "cold start" or "no depth."** The user-facing HELD reason is literally `"HELD — moment light not green (spread/depth)"` (book20_runner.py:623). The more descriptive text — *"no MEASURED per-stock ceiling yet ... unverifiable → locked, fail-closed"* — lives only inside `moment_light`'s note (desk.py:51) and is not what `_fire_lock_reason` surfaces. The "COLD-START" wording appears only in the cost readout for the half-spread fallback, a different code path: `money.py:195` — `"cited fallback %.4f (COLD-START; no depth yet)"`. So the warm-up gate works and holds, but it is not labelled "warm-up" in the held reason.

Note also two different uses of the spread number: the **lock** (moment_light) has no fallback and stays closed under 20 readings, whereas the **cost** charged on a fill (`money.half_spread`) *does* fall back to the typed 5 bps (`HALF_SPREAD = 0.0005`, money.py:19; fallback at money.py:124-130) — so even before warm-up, fills that do clear the lock are costed at the typed half-spread.

---

# Audit: do tomorrow's LIVE paper trades match the exam that produced `LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json`?

The single live order path is `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py` (its own docstring: *"THE SINGLE ORDER PATH — the 20-day book runner. This is the ONLY loop that may write a paper order."* — book20_runner.py:1). The exam that produced the pattern set is `scripts/exams_real.py` ("R-EXAMS-REAL"), promoted into the JSON by `scripts/live_pattern_set.py`.

Bottom line up front: **the live loop does NOT reproduce the certified management.** It hard-codes a 20-day hold, a 1×ATR stop, and always-on trailing/break-even/partial — while every pattern in `LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json` was certified with a 40- or 60-day hold, a **2×ATR** stop, and **trail=false** ("fixed stop and target"). Details per your five points below.

---

## 1. SIZING

**Live size = flat 1R = ₹1,000, sized so 1R ≈ one ATR of the stock.**

- `engine1_rebuild/money.py:12` — `PAPER_R_RUPEES = 1000.0  # 1R = the rupee risk per call`
- The order-writing function `fill_at_touch` does **not** compute a quantity or rupee size at all — the ledger row carries only `entry/stop/atr`, no size field (book20_runner.py:170-173). Size is materialised only in the card:
  - `engine1_rebuild/book20_runner.py:570` — `qty = max(1, round(money.PAPER_R_RUPEES / atr)) if atr else 1  # size so ~1R = the stock's own wiggle`
- The design explicitly keeps the order path flat and does **not** use the Kelly/adaptive sizer:
  - `engine1_rebuild/desk.py:20-23` — *"The 20-day book sizes every fire at a flat, shrunk 1R (standing architecture), so book20_runner never calls position_risk_rupees."*
- `adaptive_sizing.py` is **record-only** — printed on the card but never sizes the order:
  - `engine1_rebuild/adaptive_sizing.py:14` — *"adaptive never sizes on a number it does not have. RECORD-ONLY."*
  - It is only appended to the card text: book20_runner.py:583 — `card += "\nsizing · FLAT ₹%.0f · %s" % (money.PAPER_R_RUPEES, AS.explain(adaptive))`
- `flat_floor` scales with equity but for a fresh book equals ₹1,000: `adaptive_sizing.py:29-35` — `R_FRAC = PAPER_R_RUPEES / NOTIONAL_CAPITAL` (i.e. 1000/200000 = 0.5%).
- E10/E11 differ only by a sizing weight on the **same** fill (see #3), never a different price: `engine_book.py:87` — `risk = round(w * ACC.R_RUPEES, 2)`.

**The exam is pure-R, no rupee size.** `net_per_trade_R` is the mean of net-R across walk-forward trades, with no position size assumed:
- `scripts/exams_real.py:131` — `mean_net = statistics.mean(nets) if nets else 0.0`
- `scripts/exams_real.py:134` — `"mean_net_R": round(mean_net, 4)` (this is what becomes `net_per_trade_R`)
- Promotion mapping: `scripts/live_pattern_set.py:41` — `"net_per_trade_R": s.get("mean_net_R")`
- Each trade's R comes from `paper.simulate_trade(...)` and `net_R = gross_R - _cost_R(chk)` (exams_real.py:120-126) — R units only, no rupees.

**Caveat — the definition of "1R" differs between exam and live.** In the exam, 1R = `stop_atr` ATR (the certified stop, 2.0): `engine1_rebuild/paper.py:187` — `partial_R = 1.5 / sa` and paper.py:169-170 *"1R = stop_atr ATR"*. In the live loop, 1R = 1 ATR (stop is 1×ATR, see #2). So a given `net_per_trade_R` from the exam is measured against a 2-ATR risk unit, but the live book's ₹1,000 is risked over 1 ATR — the rupee meaning of the certified R is not preserved.

tool_result: 75 .claude/hooks/auto_continue.py
"""R-AUTO-CONTINUE: a Stop hook so the builder never stops on its own while checklist items remain.

It BLOCKS the stop and re-issues "continue in order" when ALL of these hold:
  - the journal's next item is non-empty (reports/BUILD-STATE.json "next"),
  - no HALT exists (E1-DESK\\HALT),
  - the last self-flag is exactly "NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD",
  - it is outside the 08:30–15:45 build pause (desk_clock.building()),
  - the checklist still has remaining items (reports/CHECKLIST.json).
Otherwise it lets the session stop — i.e. it stops for LEAD RULING NEEDED, a HALT, the checklist finished, or
the pause window. Fail-open: any error lets the stop proceed (never trap the session on a hook bug).
"""
import json
import os
import sys

REPO = os.environ.get("E1_REPO") or r"[redacted]\Desktop\trading-engines-v2"
STATE = os.environ.get("E1_STATE") or os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BUILD-STATE.json")
CHECKLIST = os.environ.get("E1_CHECKLIST") or os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CHECKLIST.json")
HALT = os.environ.get("E1_HALT_FILE") or os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK", "HALT")


def _load(p):
    try:
        return json.load(open(p, encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _building():
    try:
        sys.path.insert(0, REPO)
        from scripts import desk_clock
        return desk_clock.building()
    except Exception:
        from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
        now = datetime.now(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))
        m = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
        return m >= 15 * 60 + 45 or m < 8 * 60 + 30      # fail toward the build window (keep going)


def should_continue():
    """Continue while the checklist has work — and, per R-BACKLOG, when it empties, pull the next backlog item
    into the checklist and keep going. Stop only for a HALT, a LEAD RULING NEEDED flag, the pause window, or an
    empty checklist AND empty backlog."""
    st = _load(STATE)
    flag = (st.get("flag") or "").strip()
    if os.path.exists(HALT) or flag != "NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD" or not _building():
        return False
    remaining = [x for x in (_load(CHECKLIST).get("remaining") or []) if x]
    if remaining:
        return True
    try:                                              # checklist empty -> pull the next backlog item (R-BACKLOG)
        sys.path.insert(0, REPO)
        from scripts import backlog
        return bool(backlog.pull_next())
    except Exception:
        return False


def main():
    try:
        raw = sys.stdin.read()
    except Exception:
        raw = ""
    try:
        if should_continue():
            # block the stop and hand the session the same instruction the owner would type
            print(json.dumps({"decision": "block", "reason": "continue in order"}))
    except Exception:
        pass
    sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

B. RULING on docs/reports/2026-09-22-live-vs-exam-audit.md — R-EXAM-CONFORM. The exam is the standard; live conforms to it, never the reverse. For each of the five: management (hold length, stop multiple, trail or fixed) is read per pattern from LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json — nothing hard-coded in book20_runner.py; entry fill = the fill the exam certified (next-day open at the real opening price or worse, charges deducted); the signal is evaluated on the same bar the exam evaluated it on (if the exam used the completed daily bar at close, live evaluates once after close and queues the order for the next open — no partial-bar evaluation); exit = the certified variant, mechanical. The 20-day / 1xATR / always-trail book is a different strategy: it manages nothing in the live set until it passes the exam itself. HELD reasons name the check, including "warm-up". This is an order-path change: it goes live only through the nightly dry run + verifier rehearsal, after Part 4 exists. UNTIL PROVEN: the five evaluate and log, every signal HELD with reason "awaiting R-EXAM-CONFORM proof" — no paper order is placed under the old management. A wrong trade is not a paper trade. Where this can fail: (i) live daily bars must come from the same data source the exam used, or the twin run will show phantom signals; (ii) if proof slips past 05:00 Wed it waits a night — say so in the report, no exceptions; (iii) earliest conforming trade = the open after the first close-of-day signal following proof.

import os
import tempfile

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from engine1_rebuild import book20_runner as B, gate, paper


def _flat_daily():
    idx = pd.bdate_range("2024-01-01", periods=300)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    return pd.DataFrame({"close": 100 + rng.normal(0, 2.0, 300)}, index=idx)


def _tmp_ledgers(monkeypatch):
    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    monkeypatch.setattr(B, "LEDGER", os.path.join(d, "book20.csv"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(B, "READOUT", os.path.join(d, "readout.json"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(paper, "LEDGER", os.path.join(d, "paper.csv"))
    return d


def test_no_refire_and_unique_trade_ids(monkeypatch):
    _tmp_ledgers(monkeypatch)
    daily = _flat_daily()
    monkeypatch.setattr(B, "_candidates", lambda asof=None, reader=None: [
        {"stock": 111, "direction": "direct", "p_lb": 0.99, "sentence": "demo-pattern", "daily": daily}])
    monkeypatch.setattr(gate, "order_ok", lambda *a, **k: True)
    green = {111: {"depth": {"buy": [{"price": 99.9}], "sell": [{"price": 100.1}]}, "last_price": 100.0}}
    p1 = B.run_once(write=True, quotes=green)
    assert p1["filled_this_poll"] == 1, p1
    # poll 2: the SAME pattern on the SAME stock is still open → NO second position
    p2 = B.run_once(write=True, quotes=green)
    assert p2["filled_this_poll"] == 0, p2

"""R-LEAK-PURGE: a demo or rehearsal must write ONLY to its own throwaway log, never a live ledger.
Proves the instrument-111 leak (a demo's run_once writing to the E10/E11/adaptive ledgers) cannot recur."""
import hashlib
import os

LIVE_LEDGERS = [
    "reports/BOOK20-ADAPTIVE-LEDGER.csv",   # E1-ADAPTIVE
    "reports/E10-BOOK-LEDGER.csv",
    "reports/E11-BOOK-LEDGER.csv",
    "reports/BOOK20-LEDGER.csv",            # B6: the book20 state ledger
    "reports/ROOF-COMBINED-LEDGER.csv",     # B6: the roof ledger
]


def _snapshot():
    return {p: (hashlib.md5(open(p, "rb").read()).hexdigest() if os.path.exists(p) else None) for p in LIVE_LEDGERS}


def test_book20_demo_does_not_touch_live_ledgers():
    from engine1_rebuild import book20_runner
    before = _snapshot()
    book20_runner._demo()                        # sandboxed: every ledger it writes goes to a temp dir
    after = _snapshot()
    for p in LIVE_LEDGERS:
        assert before[p] == after[p], "demo wrote to the live ledger %s" % p


def test_rehearsal_does_not_touch_live_ledgers():
    from scripts import rehearsal_day1
    before = _snapshot()
    rehearsal_day1.rehearse_new_engines(write=False)
    after = _snapshot()
    for p in LIVE_LEDGERS:
        assert before[p] == after[p], "rehearsal wrote to the live ledger %s" % p

            continue

def _demo_body():
    # The safety-critical invariant: while HELD (no audit-pass file), run_once writes ZERO orders even if a
    # candidate would clear the required move with a green moment. We feed a synthetic candidate + green quote.
    idx = pd.bdate_range("2024-01-01", periods=300)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    daily = pd.DataFrame({"close": 100 * np.exp(np.cumsum(rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, 300)))}, index=idx)  # ~2%/day vol
    g = globals()
    _orig = g["_candidates"]
    g["_candidates"] = lambda asof=None, reader=None: [
        {"stock": 111, "direction": "direct", "p_lb": 0.99, "sentence": "demo", "daily": daily}]
    green = {111: {"depth": {"buy": [{"price": 99.9}], "sell": [{"price": 100.1}]}, "last_price": 100.0}}
    _held = gate.held
    gate.held = lambda: True                            # force the hold ON for this invariant test (the live
    #                                                    hold flag was deleted 20 Sep, so we test the property
    #                                                    directly rather than assume ambient state)
    try:
        r = run_once(write=True, quotes=green)
        assert r["would_be_orders"] >= 1, "demo should produce a would-be order that clears the required move"
        assert r["orders_written"] == 0, "HELD must write zero orders (got %s)" % r["orders_written"]
        assert r["orders_allowed_now"] is False, "order authority must be closed while held"
    finally:
        gate.held = _held
        g["_candidates"] = _orig

    import tempfile
    global LEDGER

tool_result: def record_entry(engine, trade_id, stock, side, entry, stop, family="", *, ts="", event=True):
    """Book one engine's OPEN row for a fire the flat book just took, sized weight(family) × ₹1,000 on this
    engine's own risk unit. Idempotent on trade_id. Appends a roof 'open' event (event=False in tests).
    Returns {weight, risk_rupees} or None if already booked."""
    eng = str(engine).upper()
    rows = _read(eng)
    if any(r.get("trade_id") == str(trade_id) for r in rows):
        return None
    w = weight(eng, family)
    risk = round(w * ACC.R_RUPEES, 2)
    rows.append({"trade_id": str(trade_id), "ts": ts, "status": "open", "stock": stock, "side": side,
                 "entry_price": entry, "stop": stop, "family": family, "weight": round(w, 4),
                 "risk_rupees": risk, "result_R": "", "result_rupees": "", "cause": "", "engine": eng})
    _write(eng, rows)
    if event:
        _roof_event("open", eng, _roof_tid(trade_id, eng), stock=stock, side=side, entry=entry, stop=stop)
    return {"weight": w, "risk_rupees": risk}


def record_exit(engine, trade_id, result_R, cause="", *, ts="", event=True):
    """Book the engine's EXIT: result_rupees = result_R × THIS row's own risk (never ×₹1,000). Appends a
    roof 'exit' event carrying the cause. Returns the updated row or None."""
    eng = str(engine).upper()
    rows = _read(eng)
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("trade_id") == str(trade_id) and r.get("status") == "open":
            risk = float(r.get("risk_rupees") or ACC.R_RUPEES)
            r["status"] = "exited"

    so a demo can never touch a live ledger (the instrument-111 leak). Restores the real paths afterwards."""

    try:
        from scripts import notify as _N
        _prev_ctx = _N.demo_context(True)             # R-LEAK-PURGE: drop any Telegram send during the demo
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        return fn()
    finally:
        LEDGER = saved["L"]
        _AB.LEDGER = saved["AB"]
        _paper.LEDGER = saved["P"]
        _ACC.ENGINE_LEDGERS.clear()
        _ACC.ENGINE_LEDGERS.update(saved["EL"])
        if _prev_ctx is not None:
            try:
                from scripts import notify as _N
                _N.demo_context(_prev_ctx)
            except Exception:
                pass

tool_result: def ready_line(s=None):
    """R-VITALS: the 08:30 READY line derived from the status file. Login + a green room = READY."""
    s = s or build(write=False)
    if not s["token"].get("valid"):
        return "NOT READY YET — waiting for the login."
    recorders = sum(1 for p in s["processes"] if p["name"] in
                    ("e13_depth", "chain_recorder", "xchange_recorder", "positioning_recorder"))
    runner = any(p["name"] == "book20_runner" for p in s["processes"])
    if recorders >= 1 and runner and s["moment_light_green_count"] > 0:

    return "NOT READY — %s" % (s.get("plain") or _plain(s))

tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '185,245p' scripts/liquidity_backtest.py", "description": "Read the six-item checklist function"}
tool_result: def checklist(token, entry_price, exit_price, date_str, k, k_meas, spread_scale=1.0, with_impact=True):
    """Run the six checks for one call. Returns a dict: verdict (FILLED/RESIZED/HELD), fill_shares, and the
    cost ledger (decision, fill, perfect, spread_paid, impact_paid, fees), all in ₹ where natural. PROXY-marked
    for pre-21-Sep dates. `spread_scale` multiplies the bar-range spread proxy (R-NIGHT-3 Part A calibration:
    scale = real/proxy so the charged spread matches the measured book); `with_impact=False` charges the
    spread only (the 'at touch' column)."""
    m = measures(token)
    proxy = not _has_depth(date_str)
    if not m or not entry_price:
        return {"verdict": "HELD", "reason": "no measures / price", "fill_shares": 0, "proxy": proxy}
    stop_frac = max(m["spread_band_hi"] * 3, m["sigma_min"] * 5, 0.005)   # a measured stop scale (never typed)
    want = _order_shares(entry_price, stop_frac)
    minvol = m["median_minute_volume"] or 1
    dvol = m["median_daily_volume"] or 1
    # 2. other side there / fit: the order must sit within the stock's own minute-liquidity (proxy for depth)
    fits = want <= minvol
    fill = want if fits else int(minvol)
    # 3. size vs daily volume: within a MEASURED slice (its own median minute share of the day)
    slice_cap = max(minvol, dvol * (minvol / max(dvol, 1)))              # the stock's own participation scale
    # 5. time of day normal: the entry range within the stock's measured band (proxy uses the median range)
    tod_ok = m["spread_proxy_frac"] <= m["spread_band_hi"] * 1.5
    # 6. tradeable: a non-zero move (not halted) and not a limit move (< the stock's measured daily band)
    move = abs((exit_price - entry_price) / entry_price) if (exit_price and entry_price) else 0
    tradeable = 0 < (m["median_minute_volume"] or 0) and move < max(0.18, m["sigma_min"] * 40)
    # 4. cost vs budget: spread + sqrt impact
    spread_frac = (m["spread_proxy_frac"] * spread_scale) / 2.0           # half-spread paid at the touch (scaled)
    impact_frac = (k * math.sqrt(max(fill, 1) / minvol)) if with_impact else 0.0   # sqrt law, anchored constant
    from engine1_rebuild import money
    fees_frac = money.COST_STATUTORY_RT
    cost_frac = spread_frac + impact_frac + fees_frac
    budget = _cost_budget(m)
    cost_ok = cost_frac <= budget
    if not tradeable:
        verdict, reason = "HELD", "not tradeable (halted / limit move)"
    elif not tod_ok:
        verdict, reason = "HELD", "spread outside the stock's normal band"
    elif not cost_ok:
        verdict, reason = "HELD", "cost above the measured budget"
    elif not fits:
        verdict, reason = ("RESIZED", "book too thin — cut to what fits") if fill >= 1 else ("HELD", "nothing fits")
    else:
        verdict, reason = "FILLED", "all six checks pass"
    perfect = entry_price
    fill_px = entry_price * (1 + spread_frac + impact_frac)              # buy pays up; symmetric for a sell
    return {"verdict": verdict, "reason": reason, "fill_shares": (fill if verdict != "HELD" else 0),
            "want_shares": want, "proxy": proxy,
            "decision_price": round(perfect, 2), "fill_price": round(fill_px, 2), "perfect_price": round(perfect, 2),
            "spread_paid_frac": round(spread_frac, 6), "impact_paid_frac": round(impact_frac, 6),
            "fees_frac": round(fees_frac, 6), "cost_frac": round(cost_frac, 6), "budget_frac": round(budget, 6)}


def _cost_budget(m):
    """The strategy's measured cost budget for a stock: its own typical half-spread + fees, doubled as
    headroom — measured, not typed."""
    try:
        from engine1_rebuild import money
        return (m["spread_proxy_frac"] / 2.0 + money.COST_STATUTORY_RT) * 2.0
    except Exception:
        return m["spread_proxy_frac"]

def run(write=True, limit=None):
    cost_rt, measured = calibrated_cost_rt()
    k, k_meas = LB.impact_k()
    cal = LB.spread_calibration()
    cand = evening.active_daily_direction_signals()
    reader = lambda t: ds.read(int(t), "raw")

    certs = []
    probation = []

        return {"note": "no candidates on disk", "reexamined": 0, "certified": 0}
    rows = cand.sort_values("hold_margin", ascending=False)
    if limit:
        rows = rows.head(limit)
    for _, r in rows.iterrows():
        tok = int(r["stock"]); cond = r["condition"]; metric = r["metric"]
        if not isinstance(cond, str):
            continue
        try:
            daily = TF.glue(reader(tok), "daily")
        except Exception:
            continue
        n = len(daily)
        if n < RVOL_WIN + 40:
            continue
        mask = evening._window_mask(daily, metric, cond)
        if mask is None:
            continue
        positions = list(np.flatnonzero(mask))
        direction = r["direction"]
        fam = "cross-metric" if "+" in str(metric) else (str(metric).split("[")[0].strip() or "other")
        reexamined += 1
        best = certify_candidate(tok, positions, daily, direction, fam, cost_rt, k, k_meas, cal)
        if best:
            certified += 1
            best.update({"stock": LB._sym(tok), "family": fam})
            certs.append(best)
        else:
            probation.append({"stock": LB._sym(tok), "family": fam,
                              "reason": "no management variant survived real costs + the luck correction"})
    # trades/day of the certified set = sum of fires across the kept variants over the walk-forward span

def active_daily_direction_signals():
    """Validated daily DIRECTION patterns (holdout survivors) joined to their conditions by
    pattern_id. Bounded (~the daily-D survivor set) and precise — no cross-join. The money light is
    computed inline in run_evening from each pattern's own hit-rate lower bound."""

        return pd.DataFrame()
tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '86,120p' engine1_rebuild/evening.py; echo \"=== candidate columns (return of active_daily_direction_signals) ===\"; sed -n '36,85p' engine1_rebuild/evening.py | grep -nE \"return|columns|rename|\\\"stock\\\"|\\\"metric\\\"|\\\"condition\\\"|\\\"direction\\\"|\\[\\[\" | head", "description": "Read _window_mask and candidate columns"}
tool_result: def _window_mask(daily, metric, label):
    """Boolean mask for one metric's window label on a daily frame (via finder.windows_for)."""
    try:
        series = F.metric_series(daily, metric)
    except Exception:
        return None
    for lab, mask in F.windows_for(metric, series):
        if lab == label:
            return mask.values
    return None


def _fire_today(reader, token, metric, condition, asof=None):
    """Did this daily pattern's condition fire on the latest complete daily bar?"""
    daily = TF.glue(reader(token), "daily")
    if asof is not None:
        daily = daily[daily.index <= asof]
    if len(daily) < 40:
        return False, None
    if "+" in str(metric):                       # cross-metric: "la & lb" / metric "na+nb"
        na, nb = str(metric).split("+")
        parts = str(condition).split(" & ")
        if len(parts) != 2:
            return False, None
        ma = _window_mask(daily, na, parts[0]); mb = _window_mask(daily, nb, parts[1])
        mask = (ma & mb) if (ma is not None and mb is not None) else None
    else:
        mask = _window_mask(daily, metric, condition)
    if mask is None:
        return False, None
    return bool(mask[-1]), daily

tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '/def process_eod_positions/,/^def [a-z]/p' engine1_rebuild/evening.py | head -60", "description": "Read process_eod_positions EOD flow"}
tool_result: def process_eod_positions(reader=None):
    """Advance every daily (EOD-) paper trade: a queued order fills at the next daily open once that
    bar exists; open trades are managed on daily bars to stop/target/time, else marked to market.
    Append-only: each evening adds the trade's new state as a row (the latest row per trade_id is
    its current state). Returns counts."""
    reader = reader or (lambda t: ds.read(t, "raw"))
    led = paper.read_ledger()
    n = {"filled": 0, "closed": 0, "open": 0}
    if not len(led):
        return n
    last = led.drop_duplicates("trade_id", keep="last")
    act = last[last["trade_id"].astype(str).str.startswith("EOD-") & last["status"].isin(["queued", "open"])]
    rows = []
    for _, t in act.iterrows():
        daily = TF.glue(reader(int(t["stock"])), "daily")
        qd = pd.Timestamp(str(t["queue_date"]))
        pos = int(daily.index.searchsorted(qd))
        if pos >= len(daily) or daily.index[pos].normalize() != qd:
            continue
        import re as _re
        _m = _re.search(r"next (\d+) (?:day|bar)", str(t.get("pattern", "")))
        _hz = int(_m.group(1)) if _m else None       # R-E1-PATTERN-HORIZON: the pattern's own horizon
        tr = paper.simulate_trade(daily, pos, t["direction"], t["template"], allow_open=True, horizon=_hz)
        if tr is None:
            continue                                   # next open not printed yet: still queued
        status = tr.get("status", "closed")
        if t["status"] == "queued":
            n["filled"] += 1
        n[status] += 1
        rows.append({"trade_id": t["trade_id"], "status": status, "stock": int(t["stock"]),
                     "pattern": t["pattern"], "direction": t["direction"], "template": t["template"],
                     "queue_date": t["queue_date"], "entry_date": tr["entry_date"],
                     "entry_price": tr["entry_price"], "stop": tr.get("stop"), "target": tr.get("target"),
                     "exit_date": tr.get("exit_date", ""), "exit_price": tr.get("exit_price", ""),
                     "exit_reason": tr.get("exit_reason", ""), "cost_R": tr["cost_R"],
                     "result_R": tr.get("result_R", ""), "result_rupees": tr.get("result_rupees", ""),
                     "mark_R": tr.get("mark_R", ""), "ts": "evening"})
    if rows:
        paper.append_rows(rows)
    return n

tool_result: def _with_link(message: str) -> str:
    """R-EYES-COMPLETE §7: EVERY Telegram line ends with the mirror link (the build room — one link that shows
    Now/Journal/Commits/Tests/Supervisor/Messages/Reports/Screenshots/Queue/Eyes). A line that already carries
    an e1-live-site link (the beat/live-bar carry the status page, which the mirror contains) is not doubled.
    (Supersedes the earlier status-only rule for alert lines.)"""
    m = str(message)
    if "github.io/e1-live-site" in m:
        return m
    return m.rstrip() + " · " + MIRROR_LINK

    """R-E1-NETTING order path: merge same stock+bucket fires with desk.net_orders, then build the queue
    rows (respecting MAX_OPEN, one open per stock). A LONE fire passes through UNCHANGED (byte-identical
    to the pre-netting first-wins path); 2+ on one stock+bucket net to one strength-weighted order
    (opposing fires cancel in proportion to their measured strength, a full cancel places no order).
    R-E1-THREE-ENGINE item 3: `clusters` ({stock: cluster_id}) enforces cluster=ONE-position — a new entry
    whose correlation cluster already holds an open position is refused. No cluster map (the RAM wall /
    cold start) -> sized on its own (paper-phase generosity, CLAUDE.md §19.1). Mutates open_stocks with
    each stock it queues; returns the new queue rows. R-E1-THREE-ENGINE PCA alarm: book_one_bet=True (the
assistant: net_and_queue is complex (netting/clusters). For a clean, correct threading of per-pattern management (horizon/stop_atr/trail) into the one fill function, let me check `paper.simulate_trade`'s stop_atr/manage semantics — that determines whether I can pass LIVE-PATTERN-SET's values directly:
tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '77,135p' engine1_rebuild/paper.py", "description": "Read simulate_trade stop_atr/manage semantics"}
tool_result: def simulate_trade(daily: pd.DataFrame, signal_pos: int, direction: str, template: str,
                   allow_open: bool = False, horizon: int = None, manage: bool = False,
                   stop_atr: float = 1.0):
    """Enter at the NEXT daily open after signal_pos; exit per template on later daily bars.
    Returns a dict (entry/stop/target/exit/result_R) or None if not fillable (end of data / flat ATR).
    allow_open=True (the live evening ledger): a trade whose exit has not happened on the bars that
    exist yet comes back status 'open' with a mark-to-market, instead of a premature time-exit.
    horizon (R-E1-PATTERN-HORIZON): the holding period is the PATTERN'S OWN horizon when given, never the
    template's fixed max-hold. horizon=None keeps the old behaviour byte-for-byte (the template's maxH).
    manage (R-E1-DESK rules 1/2/4): when True the exit is the DESK's live management — break-even after
    +1 ATR, then trail 1 ATR behind, and a half PARTIAL at +1.5 ATR (desk.manage_stop) — instead of the
    flat template stop/target. manage=False keeps the old template behaviour byte-for-byte."""
    name, sm, tm, maxH = next(t for t in TEMPLATES if t[0] == template)
    maxH = int(horizon) if horizon else maxH        # pattern's own holding period, not a shared template
    if manage:
        return _simulate_managed(daily, signal_pos, direction, maxH, allow_open, stop_atr=stop_atr)
    atr = _atr(daily).values
    o = daily["open"].values; h = daily["high"].values; lo = daily["low"].values; c = daily["close"].values
    idx = daily.index
    e = signal_pos + 1                         # next bar's open = the fill
    if e >= len(daily) or not (atr[signal_pos] > 0):
        return None
    a = atr[signal_pos]
    if a <= c[e] * MIN_ATR_FRAC:
        return None
    entry = o[e]
    long = (direction == "direct")             # direct D = long; inverse D = short
    unresolved = allow_open and (e + maxH > len(daily) - 1)   # exit day not printed yet
    if name == "time5":
        if unresolved:
            return _open(idx, e, entry, c[-1], a, long, None, None)
        j = min(e + maxH, len(daily) - 1)
        r = (c[j] - entry) / a
        r = float(np.clip(r if long else -r, -CLIP_R, CLIP_R))
        return _trade(idx, e, j, entry, c[j], "time", r, a, long, sm=None, tm=None)
    stop = entry - sm * a if long else entry + sm * a
    tgt = entry + tm * a if long else entry - tm * a
    for j in range(e + 1, min(e + maxH + 1, len(daily))):
        if long:
            if lo[j] <= stop:
                return _trade(idx, e, j, entry, stop, "stop", -1.0, a, long, sm, tm)
            if h[j] >= tgt:
                return _trade(idx, e, j, entry, tgt, "target", tm / sm, a, long, sm, tm)
        else:
            if h[j] >= stop:
                return _trade(idx, e, j, entry, stop, "stop", -1.0, a, long, sm, tm)
            if lo[j] <= tgt:
                return _trade(idx, e, j, entry, tgt, "target", tm / sm, a, long, sm, tm)
    if unresolved:
        return _open(idx, e, entry, c[-1], a, long, sm, tm)
    j = min(e + maxH, len(daily) - 1)          # time-exit fallback
    r = float(np.clip((c[j] - entry) / a if long else -(c[j] - entry) / a, -CLIP_R, CLIP_R))
    return _trade(idx, e, j, entry, c[j], "time", r, a, long, sm, tm)


STOP_PCTL = 90          # R-E1-PATTERN-STOP: keep ~90% of CORRECT predictions alive (declared quantile)
STOP_MIN_CORRECT = 20   # need at least this many correct train/test fires to measure a pattern stop
